# P01/I03/S01/C15 experiment disposition ledger

- baseline: `92cdd9114046115d45451153c6ebad3b425db36e`
- experiment X1: 9488f2000f0a31485679c39a568ff2a6d9d6f28f; first parent fa9c738577110c893b7b156c0e68b68fdeec6e51.
- experiment X2: 4225fae1466aef093f5f22f69bd8c17c6e4420d7; first parent 93fbc8e59f4b58a26520915ac1285d3fe8f54622.
- Extraction used both exact function-context and U0 diffs plus both exact diff-tree manifests.
- Each U0 side maps to its smallest enclosing fully qualified item and every declaration beginning in that side. This refines the initial file-global 498-name result to 511 fully qualified occurrences; 498 is audit history, not accepted coverage.
- Every surviving direct caller of an experiment-introduced descendant is recorded, including introduced helper-to-helper and adjacent governing callers.
- Relationship extraction is receiver/type/module qualified. Method calls use the concrete receiver type from compiler THIR; associated and free calls use the resolved local definition path. A shared simple name is never evidence.
- The re-extraction removed 757 simple-name collision rows (`finish`: 81, `try_new`: 500, `from`: 174, `run`: 2) and retained stable IDs for the 400 compiler-resolved relationships.
- Decisions use ownership, cohesion, order, lifetime, failure, invariant, and test intent. Size signals are not a disposition rationale.

The three review-proven collisions resolve respectively to
`gpu_transaction::GpuOperationTransaction::finish`,
`error::RuntimeCapabilityUnavailable::try_new`, and `usize::from`.
Representative cross-module collisions also remove
`vello_engine::recording::RecordingBuilder::finish` and the two unrelated
text-model `.run()` calls.

## Rationale codes

- R-GOVERNING-FLOW: existing orchestration retains order, cleanup, and diagnostics while delegating.
- R-OPERATION-PHASE: one validation, lowering, encoding, acquisition, publication, or failure phase.
- R-MODEL: one coherent phase, invariant, or ownership state.
- R-TEST-INTENT: one focused fixture, oracle, assertion, or source-contract check.
- R-OWNERSHIP: the import or impl remains with its governing module or type.
- R-RELATIONSHIP: the call preserves the current phase, order, lifetime, or test-intent handoff.
- R-MECHANICAL-OWNERSHIP: tuple-return or lexical-parser fragmentation moved coupled state or logic away from its owner.
- R-RELATIONSHIP-REMEDIATION: the call touches that same open ownership repair.
- R-SUPERSEDED-DUPLICATE: X2 c10_future_backdrop_scene is absent; X1 c10_future_backdrop_scene_for_test survives with that purpose.
- R-SUPERSEDED-ABSENCE: later behavior, test, or import evolution removed the historical item and no helper relationship survives.
